@FundaAI（保留 2 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 2 条）

2026-11-09 09:41 北京时间 ｜ Source: @FundaAI ｜ 原帖链接

周报｜关于短/中/长期、德州许可、网络安全、APP 与 Unity、太空算力、光通信需求的思考

在近年来最剧烈的市场抛售之一之后，我们想退后一步，从不同时间维度审视 AI 板块。

长期来看，我们对 AI 的潜力依然感到兴奋。前沿实验室在通往 AGI 的道路上持续取得有意义的进展，更大的预训练模型带来显著的能力提升。随着强化学习的规模化和数据的积累，通用模型也在扩展到以前由专用模型服务的领域，包括机器人以及视频和音频生成。在我们与顶尖研究者的交流中，对 2028 年或之后实现 AGI 的乐观程度高于我们以往所见。

中期图景（大致到 2027 年）更难评估。领先实验室的大部分收入仍来自 AI 初创公司和大型科技公司。对于依赖风险投资的初创公司，如果宏观条件和融资可得性恶化，它们的支出可持续性如何？大型科技公司已经在优化 token 消耗：它们的支出会稳定在哪里，什么会推动重新加速？与此同时，我们的企业调研一直显示采用速度慢于市场的期望。综合来看，这些趋势提出了一种可能性：投资者预期的支出增长与客户最终交付之间可能存在显著差距。

在半导体和硬件方面，我们听到越来越多来自亚洲供应链的交付和采购进度放缓的报告。这些值得关注，但尚不能确认砍单。它们主要是产品切换和部署时间的结果，还是支出纪律收紧的早期迹象？如果融资条件进一步收紧，尤其是对 neocloud 而言，基础设施投资会有多大韧性？

这些中期问题可以与近期反弹并存。在如此剧烈的抛售之后，如果股价已经反映了弱得多的支出前景，即使第四季度收入预期的适度改善也可能产生影响。一个强有力的模型发布可能提供更可信的催化剂，特别是如果更好的能力转化为更强的付费需求和更持久的定价。我们不做市场方向判断。我们确实认为，随着预期重置和新证据出现，保持开放心态很重要。

也有一些值得密切关注的新领域。我们最新的太空算力研究是一个亮点：散热设计、供应链订单和发射规划方面的进展正使轨道算力成为更具体的工程命题。在我们的基准情景中，到 2028 年底在轨容量约 0.5 GW，乐观情景下约 1 GW，散热验证和发射节奏仍是关键。网络安全是我们看到巨大机会的另一个领域。AI 正在加速漏洞发现，并增加对防御、修复和治理的需求。需求在增长，但变现时机很重要：更多的攻击未必会立即转化为上市安全厂商的收入提升。

在 AI 基础设施交易之外，AppLovin 和 Unity 提醒我们其他地方仍有机会。我们在数个季度里一直密切跟踪这两家公司，我们的渠道调研捕捉到了它们竞争格局和经营势头的重要变化。它们强劲的业绩和财报后的上涨强化了这种持续研究的价值。我们将继续在覆盖范围内寻找和跟踪机会，包括那些在市场主导性 AI 叙事之外的机会。

本周报告

LLM 26Q4：能力进步快于商业回报。我们的深度报告考察前沿竞争、任务成本下降和 AI 辅助研究，领先实验室约 70% 的算力支出仍用于付费推理以外的用途。

太空算力：工程进展使轨道容量更加切实。我们的供应链研究识别出 6000 个计划机架，以及到 2028 年底基准情景约 0.5 GW、乐观情景约 1 GW 在轨算力的路径，取决于发射节奏和散热验证。

网络安全：客户仍偏好现有的云和安全平台。我们最新的调研显示修复、响应和治理是支出的核心，尽管 AI 驱动的漏洞发现在加快，近期预算提升有限。

德州 AIDC：选举后许可不确定性上升。我们暂时维持 2027 年交付预测，但在下行情景中，长期冻结可能使可交付供应再减少 2–3 GW 的 IT 容量。

前瞻与回顾｜APP 与 Unity 26Q3：两者都在竞争纠纷中增长。继我们关于竞价和数据访问的渠道调研之后，AppLovin 以 55% 的收入增长重回超预期并上调指引，而 Unity 的 Vector 环比增长 40%，指引约为 20%。

回顾｜DDOG 26Q3：定价和承诺使指引复杂化。收入增长 35.2%，非 AI 增长连续第五个季度加速，但最低承诺和更低的续约定价使第四季度增长指引保持在 25–26%。

回顾｜PLTR FY26Q3：增长保持强劲，订单走软。收入增长 96%，第四季度指引意味着约 88% 的增长；更短的合同和重新定价灵活性为订单走软提供了可能的解释，但并不能确认潜在需求变弱。

INIO：装机基础支撑长期服务尾部。我们的首次覆盖报告考察约 44 GW 的装机容量、80 亿美元的设备积压订单和 10–15 年的服务关系，以及交付执行和 11 月的禁售期到期。

详细报告（链接见原帖）

原文：Weekly|Thoughts on Short/Mid/Long Terms, Texas Permitting, Cybersecurity, APP & Unity, Space Compute, Optical Demand

After one of the most violent market selloffs in recent years, we want to take a step back and consider the AI sector across different time horizons.

Over the long term, we remain excited about AI’s potential. Frontier labs continue to make meaningful progress toward AGI, with larger pretrained models delivering substantial capability gains. As reinforcement learning scales and data accumulates, general-purpose models are also expanding into areas previously served by specialized models, including robotics and video and audio generation. In our conversations with leading researchers, optimism about reaching AGI in 2028 or beyond is higher than we have previously encountered.

The medium-term picture, through roughly 2027, is harder to assess. Much of the leading labs’ revenue still comes from AI startups and large technology companies. For startups dependent on venture funding, how sustainable is their spending if macro conditions and funding availability deteriorate? Large technology companies are already optimizing token consumption: where does their spending settle, and what would drive renewed acceleration? Meanwhile, our enterprise surveys have consistently pointed to slower adoption than the market had hoped for. Taken together, these trends raise the possibility of a meaningful gap between the spending growth investors expect and what customers ultimately deliver.

On the semiconductor and hardware side, we are hearing more reports from the Asian supply chain of slower deliveries and procurement schedules. These warrant attention, though they do not yet establish order cuts. Are they mainly the result of product transitions and deployment timing, or early signs of tighter spending discipline? And how resilient would infrastructure investment prove if financing conditions tightened further, particularly for neoclouds?

These medium-term questions can coexist with a near-term rebound. Following such a sharp selloff, even modest improvements in fourth-quarter revenue expectations could matter if share prices already discount a much weaker spending outlook. A strong model release could provide a more credible catalyst, particularly if better capabilities translate into stronger paid demand and more durable pricing. We are not making a market-direction call. We do think it is important to remain open-minded as expectations reset and new evidence emerges.

There are also new areas worth watching closely. Our latest space compute work is one bright spot: progress in thermal design, supply-chain orders and launch planning is making orbital compute a more concrete engineering proposition. We see a path to roughly 0.5 GW of on-orbit capacity by the end of 2028 in our base case, and approximately 1 GW in an optimistic scenario, with thermal validation and launch cadence still critical. Cybersecurity remains another area where we see substantial opportunity. AI is accelerating vulnerability discovery and increasing the demands on defense, remediation and governance. The need is growing, but the timing of monetization matters: more attacks will not necessarily translate into an immediate revenue uplift for listed security vendors.

Beyond the AI infrastructure trade, AppLovin and Unity are a reminder that opportunities remain elsewhere. We have followed both companies closely through several quarters, and our channel work has captured important shifts in their competitive dynamics and operating momentum. Their strong results and post-earnings rallies reinforce the value of that sustained research. We will continue looking for and tracking opportunities across our coverage, including those outside the market’s dominant AI narrative.

@Roblox_RTC（保留 2 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 6 条）

2026-11-09 18:08 北京时间 ｜ Source: @Roblox_RTC ｜ 原帖链接

我们深入分析了 Lunar for Roblox 的泄露版本。

默认情况下，Lunar 跟踪应用中发生的至少 64 种不同操作。每 15 秒，它们就把每次交互发送到服务器。包括：
• 你加入的每个游戏以及游玩时长
• 你收藏、点赞或点踩的每个游戏
• 你开关通知的每个游戏
• 你加入/退出的每个群组
• 你创建/修改/激活的每个宏
• 你创建、删除、收藏或复制的每张截图/剪辑
• 你发布的每个社区论坛帖子/回复

根据 Roblox 自己关于用户隐私和第三方集成的政策，“创作者不得出于自身商业目的将个人用户数据发送给社交平台、聊天应用、广告网络、供应商或合作伙伴。创作者也不得出售、出租、租赁或转让这些数据。”

通过 Overwolf 披露，超过一千家外部广告供应商可以出于以下目的访问用户数据：
• 个性化广告（789 家供应商）
• 个性化内容（275 家供应商）
• 广告和内容衡量及受众研究（672 家供应商）
• 在设备上存储和/或访问信息（1005 家供应商）

Lunar 允许你关闭其自身的分析和“广告优化”，两者默认开启。但应用内置的第三方服务更难控制。报告崩溃和性能数据的 Sentry 没有关闭选项。Overwolf 的广告控制限制广告定向，但广告本身无法关闭。

在 Windows 上，Overwolf 会读取 Opera 和 Opera GX（如已安装）保存的 cookie，并将其复制到 Overwolf 内置的广告浏览器中，跳过 Google、Facebook、Steam 和 Amazon 等主要网站。这可能让应用中显示的广告网络从你的正常浏览中识别你。它还会检查杀毒软件，如果发现则似乎跳过这一步，而启动它的代码不检查用户同意。Overwolf 还运行自己的匿名分析，应用可以通过一次调用关闭它。Lunar 从未这样做，也没有提供这个设置。

Lunar 目前包含与 Overwolf 广告系统相连的代码，可将电子邮件地址转换为无盐哈希（MD5、SHA-1 和 SHA-256）。这些哈希通常用于在不同应用和网站之间识别同一个人。这个版本中没有任何东西运行该代码，而且 Lunar 无法获取你的电子邮件，所以目前没有收集电子邮件数据，但未来的更新可能会启用它，因为其隐私政策中提到了这一点。

此外，Lunar 有内部开关，可以阻止特定用户（在应用内）购买虚拟形象物品、购买私人服务器、使用宏、多开启动、使用按游戏设置的服装以及查看广告。

原文：We dug into the leaked build of Lunar for Roblox.

By default, Lunar tracks at least 64 different kinds of actions that occur in the app. Every 15 seconds, they send every interaction to their servers. This includes:
• Every game you join and how long you played
• Every game you favorite, like, or dislike
• Every game you toggle notifications for
• Every group you join/leave
• Every macro you create/modify/activate
• Every screenshot/clip you create, delete, favorite, or copy
• Every community forum post/reply you make

According to Roblox's own policy about User Privacy and Third-Party Integrations, "Creators may not send individual user data to social platforms, chat applications, advertising networks, vendors, or partners for their own business purposes. Creators also may not sell, rent, lease, or syndicate this data."

Through Overwolf, it's disclosed that over a thousand external ad vendors can access user data for purposes including:
• Personalized advertising (789 vendors)
• Personalized content (275 vendors)
• Advertising and content measurement, and audience research (672 vendors)
• Store and/or access information on a device (1005 vendors)

Lunar lets you turn off its own analytics and "ad optimization," both on by default. But the third-party services built into the app are harder to control. Sentry, which reports crashes and performance data, has no setting to turn it off. Overwolf's ad controls limit ad targeting, but the ads themselves can't be turned off.

On Windows, Overwolf reads the cookies saved by Opera and Opera GX (if installed) and copies them into Overwolf's built-in ad browser, skipping major sites like Google, Facebook, Steam, and Amazon. This likely lets ad networks shown in the app recognize you from your normal browsing. It also checks for antivirus software and appears to skip this step if it finds one, and the code that starts it doesn't check for consent. Overwolf also runs its own anonymous analytics, which apps can turn off with one call. Lunar never does this or offers it as a setting.

Lunar currently contains code, connected to the Overwolf ad system, that turns an email address into unsalted hashes (MD5, SHA-1, and SHA-256). These hashes are commonly used to recognize the same person across different apps and websites. Nothing in this version runs that code, and Lunar has no way to get your email, so no email data is collected today, but a future update could switch it on, as it's mentioned in their privacy policy.

Also, Lunar has internal kill switches that could prevent certain users from (within the app) purchasing avatar items, purchasing private servers, using macros, multi-instance launching, using per-game outfits, and viewing ads.

2026-11-09 18:08 北京时间 ｜ Source: @Roblox_RTC ｜ 原帖链接

（补充说明）本报告基于我们自己的分析。它描述的是已发布代码及捆绑的第三方组件（如 Overwolf）被设计用来做什么，而不是任何人的意图；服务器端行为和部分运行时细节无法评估；后续应用版本可能有所不同。

我们还想补充，当前版本提供专门针对特定游戏的预制宏，如 Grow a Garden、Blox Fruits、Fisch 和 Pet Simulator 99。每个游戏中宏是被允许、警告还是阻止，由来自 Lunar 服务器的一个列表决定。不在列表中的游戏默认为警告，宏仍会运行。应用不显示 Lunar 如何决定每个游戏的设置。

原文：This report is based on our own analysis. It describes what the shipped code and bundled third-party components such as Overwolf are built to do, not anyone's intent; server-side behavior and some runtime details couldn't be assessed; later app versions may differ.

We also want to add that the current build offers pre-built macros dedicated to specific games, like Grow a Garden, Blox Fruits, Fisch, and Pet Simulator 99. Whether macros are allowed, warned about, or blocked in each game is set by a list from Lunar's servers. Games not on that list default to a warning, and macros still run. The app doesn't show how Lunar decides each game's setting.

[Anthropic/OpenAI] 2026-11-09 12:04 北京时间 ｜ Source: @long_term_pick ｜ 原帖链接

（long_term_pick）更便宜的 token 并不意味着更小的 AI 账单。据 a16z 与 Ornn 数据，自 5 月以来混合 token 价格下降了 67%，而据 Ramp 的信用卡数据，token 支出增长了 122%。

价格下降部分来自开源权重模型。在 Vercel 的 AI Gateway 上，它们从一年前占 token 量的 2–3% 升至 10 月 10 日的 61%，但据 Baird 只占支出的约 30%。Baird 认为 Palantir（$PLTR）是赢家，因为其平台适用于任何模型。

闭源模型仍然拿走大部分钱。在 OpenRouter 上，最近一周 Anthropic 占 OpenAI + Anthropic 支出的 66%，token 占 48%。OpenAI 曾在 8 月下旬短暂超过 Anthropic 几周，现在为 34%。

原文：Cheaper #tokens do not mean smaller #AI bills. Since May, the blended token price is down 67%, according to a16z with Ornn data, while token spend increased 122% by Ramp's card data.

Part of the price drop is open-weight models. On #Vercel's AI Gateway, they went from 2-3% of token volume a year ago to 61% on Oct 10, but only ~30% of spend, by Baird. Baird sees #Palantir ($PLTR) as a winner here, because its platform works with any model.

The closed models still take most of the money. On #OpenRouter, Anthropic had 66% of #OpenAI + #Anthropic spend in the latest week, with 48% of the tokens. OpenAI passed Anthropic for a few weeks in late August, and is now at 34%.

[HBM/DRAM/NAND] 2026-11-09 10:48 北京时间 ｜ Source: @StragglerLiu ｜ 原帖链接

（StragglerLiu）SK 海力士的高带宽闪存（HBF）目标是约 14 倍 HBM 容量、约一半带宽。

但更重要的进展不是标题规格，而是 AI 推理可能不再需要每个字节都放在最昂贵的层级。

长上下文推理和多轮 agent 产生巨大的 KV cache。有些数据需要 GPU 即时访问，其他数据可以存得更远、需要时再取回。

HBF、池化 DRAM 和共享 SSD 是利用这一区别的不同尝试。

投资含义不是 NAND 取代 HBM，而是存储经济性可能越来越取决于系统在层级间移动数据的效率，而不仅仅是安装了多少 HBM。

这可能扩大 NAND 和存储系统供应商的机会，同时使互连带宽、检索延迟和软件调度变得更重要。

SK 海力士目标在 2028 年初提供 HBF 样品。商业化采用尚未得到验证。

真正的检验是每个成功服务 token 的成本，包括存储、数据移动、延迟和功耗——而不仅仅是单芯片容量。

原文：SK hynix is targeting roughly 14x HBM capacity at about half the bandwidth with High Bandwidth Flash.

But the more important development isn't the headline specification.

It's the possibility that AI inference will no longer need every byte of memory to sit in the most expensive tier.

Long-context inference and multi-turn agents create enormous KV caches. Some data needs immediate GPU access. Other data can be stored farther away and retrieved when needed.

HBF, pooled DRAM and shared SSDs are different attempts to exploit that distinction.

The investment implication isn't that NAND replaces HBM.

It's that memory economics may increasingly depend on how effectively systems move data between tiers, rather than simply how much HBM they install.

That could expand the opportunity for NAND and memory-system suppliers while making interconnect bandwidth, retrieval latency and software scheduling more important.

SK hynix targets HBF samples in early 2028. Commercial adoption remains unproven.

The real test is cost per successfully served token, including memory, data movement, latency and power—not capacity per chip alone.

[HBM/DRAM/NAND] 2026-11-09 12:52 北京时间 ｜ Source: @rokhardsolid ｜ 原帖链接

（rokhardsolid）存储关乎的是量，而不只是价格，而后段封装是最先体现的地方。

Newspim（11 月 9 日）援引 Growth Research 报告：芯片厂商把内部封装空间、设备和人员优先给 HBM，把通用存储的后段推给外部 OSAT，越来越多以封装、测试和模组组装的交钥匙形式。Hana Micron 第二季度营收同比增长 100.8%，SFA Semicon 增长 65.9%，Apact 增长 69.5%。Hana Micron 与 SK 海力士签有到 2029 年的 DRAM/NAND 后段交钥匙协议，并计划在 2026 年底至 2027 年间将越南北江产能从每月 1.5 亿颗提升至 2 亿颗。其第二季度营业利润为 1423 亿韩元，增长 371.1%。报告还认为，SOCAMM2 采用的 12 层 LPDDR 堆叠每个封装的键合线用量将增加 50% 以上，利好 MK Electron。

如果外包份额上升的同时存储产量上升，OSAT 的量会比存储位元增长更快。很多分析师都这么说。他们预计这一顺风将持续到 2028 年上半年新的通用 DRAM 产能落地，所以要关注 Hana Micron 的越南爬坡，以及三星是否跟进更大的交钥匙协议。

原文：Memory's is about volume, not just price, and back-end packaging is where it shows up first.

Newspim (Nov 9) on a Growth Research report: chipmakers are giving in-house packaging space, tools and staff to HBM first, which pushes commodity memory back-end to outside OSATs, increasingly as turnkey packaging, test and module assembly. Q2 revenue rose 100.8% YoY at Hana Micron, 65.9% at SFA Semicon and 69.5% at Apact. Hana Micron has a turnkey DRAM/NAND back-end deal with SK hynix through 2029 and plans to lift its Bac Giang, Vietnam capacity from 150M to 200M units a month between late 2026 and 2027. Its Q2 operating profit was ₩142.3B, up 371.1%. The report also sees bonding wire use per package rising 50%+ at 12-high LPDDR stacks for SOCAMM2, a tailwind for MK Electron.

If outsourcing share rises while memory output rises, OSAT volumes grow faster than memory bits. That's what a lot of the analysts are saying. They expect the tailwind to last until new commodity DRAM capacity lands in 1H28, so watch Hana Micron's Vietnam ramp and whether Samsung follows with bigger turnkey deals.

[MU/SNDK/SKHY] 2026-11-09 01:27 北京时间 ｜ Source: @MarcelLarsen_ ｜ 原帖链接

（MarcelLarsen_，回应 @jukan05 关于存储 LTA 可能像 onsemi/Microchip 那样被违约的担忧）@jukan05，我查阅了你所举例子背后的电话会记录和文件。它们站得住脚。问题在于模板。onsemi 和 Microchip 被打破的合同与存储厂商今年签署的合同结构不同，而差异恰恰落在你担心的地方：价格和延期。

1. 价格
onsemi 的 LTSA 是固定价格。在 2023 年 5 月的分析师日上，El-Khoury 称其为“量和价都锁定”的多年期协议。高于市场的固定价格必然引发重新谈判之争。

存储 LTA 大多是浮动的。美光 10-K 称其大多数 SCA 的定价“受最低和最高区间约束”，约四分之一的 SCA 收入定期协商或随市场变动。SK 海力士称其 LTA“在与客户约定的价格区间内反映市场状况”。所以大部分合同量会随市场下调重新定价直到触及底价，没人声称 80% 的利润率被锁定。

锁定的是底价。Mark Murphy 9 月表示：“即使在底价，我们预计利润率也将显著高于以往任何周期的峰值利润率。”美光 2018 年峰值毛利率为 61.4%，2023 财年第二季度为 -31.4%。其约 1500 亿美元的 RPO“基于承诺数量和最低定价”。

2. 延期与抵押
Microchip 的 PSP 是 12 个月的订单，“在价格上涨时”可取消，其 10-K 自己说它“不是供应保证”。那是一个优先队列。其实际 LTSA 在 2025 年 9 月有 2.914 亿美元客户押金。

• 美光：“所有 SCA 都有照付不议的合同量。”320 亿美元客户财务承诺，“其中绝大部分是现金押金”。押金余额在一个季度内从 5.68 亿美元升至 129 亿美元。仅增量就约是 Microchip 全部 LTSA 押金余额的 42 倍（我的计算）

• SanDisk：主要是由第三方金融机构持有的财务担保，95% 的交易对手为投资级

• SK 海力士：与 14 家关键客户签有 LTA，内置押金作为保障

在有数十亿抵押品绑定的情况下延期交货，远比推迟一张采购订单的地位弱。

3. 需求转向时谁有议价权
onsemi 的碳化硅削减来自一家 OEM，William Blair 点名是 Tesla，Tesla 当年早些时候称找到了在下一代动力总成中少用 75% 碳化硅的方法。碳化硅有其他供应商，而 Microchip 的客户在消化过剩库存。对双方来说妥协都是理性的。

今天的存储：
• SK 海力士：DRAM 和 HBM 到 2029 年“实际上已售罄”，LTA 覆盖 2027 和 2028 年超过 50% 的产能
• 三星：已签 10 份 LTA，另有 10 份在谈，并且“收到对现有合同已覆盖期间的额外供应请求”
• SanDisk：约 50% 的 2027 年位元和约三分之二的 2028 年已签约，管道需求到本世纪末都高于供给
• 美光：“客户希望 SCA 保障供应超过 2030 年”，已签署至 2031 年的 SCA
如果一家超大规模云厂商延期而其他厂商都在配额中，那部分量就会转售给排队者。

4. 押金改变了周期
你说预付款是最可靠的部分，因为它们为产能提供资金。同意，而这才是更大的事。过去的存储崩盘始于供应商在峰值时用自己的资产负债表为产能融资。现在客户为自己承诺要接收的产能预付资金，这把新供给与已签署的需求绑定在一起。

5. 数千亿美元
没人按 80% 永续定价。美光 690 美元约为其 2026 财年第一季度 EPS 指引年化的 4.5 倍，较高点低 45%。SK 海力士周五下跌 10% 后约为过往盈利的 5 倍。

我同意的地方：在真正的下行中，未签约部分会重新定价，合同价格向底价滑落，部分延期会被协商。但美光的底价高于以往每个周期的峰值利润率，SK 海力士和 SanDisk 有一半或更多的 2027–28 年供给已签约且有抵押支撑，而上一次下行把美光带到了 -31.4%。

原文：$MU $SNDK $SKHY

@jukan05 , I went through the transcripts and filings behind your examples. They hold up. The problem is the template. The contracts onsemi and Microchip bent were built differently from what the memory makers signed this year, and the differences land on exactly what you're worried about: price and deferrals.

1. Price
onsemi's LTSAs were fixed-price. At its May 2023 analyst day El-Khoury described them as multiyear deals where "both volume and pricing is locked in." A fixed price above market guarantees a renegotiation fight.

Memory LTAs mostly float. Micron's 10-K says the majority of its SCAs have pricing "subject to minimum and maximum bands," and about a quarter of SCA revenue is negotiated periodically or moves with the market. SK hynix says its LTAs "reflect market conditions within a price range agreed upon with the customer." So most contracted volume reprices down with the market until it hits the floor, and nobody is claiming 80% margins are locked in.

What's locked is the floor. Mark Murphy in September: "even at floor prices, we expect margins meaningfully above any prior cycle peak margins." Micron's 2018 peak gross margin was 61.4%. In fiscal Q2 2023 it was -31.4%. Its ~$150B of RPO "is based on committed volumes and minimum pricing."

2. Deferrals and collateral
Microchip's PSP was 12 months of orders, cancellable "in the event of price increases," and its own 10-K said it "is not a guarantee of supply." That's a priority queue. Its actual LTSAs had $291.4M of customer deposits in September 2025.

• Micron: "All SCAs have take or pay contracted volumes." $32B of customer financial commitments, "the vast majority of which are cash deposits." The deposit balance went from $568M to $12.9B in one quarter. The increase alone is ~42x Microchip's whole LTSA deposit balance (my math)

• SanDisk: mostly financial guarantees held by third-party financial institutions, with 95% of counterparties investment grade

• SK hynix: LTAs with 14 key customers, deposits built in as safeguards

Deferring volume with billions of collateral tied to it is a much weaker position than pushing out a purchase order.

3. Who had leverage when demand turned
onsemi's SiC cut came from one OEM, and William Blair named Tesla, which had said earlier that year it found a way to use 75% less silicon carbide in its next powertrain. SiC had other suppliers, and Microchip's customers were working down excess inventory. Bending was the rational move for both.

Memory today:
• SK hynix: DRAM and HBM "effectively sold out" through 2029, LTAs covering over 50% of 2027 and 2028 capacity

• Samsung: 10 LTAs signed, 10 more in talks, and "receiving requests for additional supply for periods that are already covered under existing contracts"

• SanDisk: ~50% of 2027 bits and ~two-thirds of 2028 contracted, pipeline above supply through the end of the decade

• Micron: "Customers want SCA assured supply beyond 2030," with SCAs already signed into 2031
If one hyperscaler defers while the rest are on allocation, that volume gets resold to the queue.

4. The deposits change the cycle
You called the prepayments the most reliable part because they fund capacity. Agreed, and that's the bigger deal. Past memory busts started with suppliers funding capacity on their own balance sheets at the peak. Now customers prefund capacity they've committed to take, which ties new supply to signed demand.

5. Hundreds of billions
Nobody is pricing 80% forever. Micron at $690 is ~4.5x its fiscal Q1 EPS guide annualized and 45% below its high. SK hynix is ~5x trailing earnings after falling 10% on Friday.

Where I agree: in a real downturn the uncontracted share reprices, contract prices slide toward the floor and some deferrals get negotiated. But Micron's floor sits above every prior cycle peak margin, SK hynix and SanDisk have half or more of 2027-28 supply contracted with collateral behind it, and the last downturn took Micron to -31.4%.

（ALikhodedov）存储下行

关于 LTA 的持久性以及 HBM 和定制存储能在多大程度上抵御存储周期，有很多争论。

There is a lot of debate on durability of LTAs and degree to which HBM and custom memory will provide protection against the memory cycle.

My 2c in relation to the investment decision (whether to own/buy SK Hynix & Samsung at current prices)

- at the moment the market does not even want to pay 2027-28 projected earnings for these two stocks.
